## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else, and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요, 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡겨 주세요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 고쳐 줘"처럼 말씀하시면 작업을 열어서 끝나면 알려 드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```

  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK

```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP は保証せず速さと軽さを優先します。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla: ordena o junta tus commits y mantiene el historial lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la usan otras personas, porque reescribir commits compartidos rompe su trabajo y merge conserva el historial tal como ocurrió.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据按序、不丢不重地送到；UDP 是无连接的尽力投递，速度快、开销小，但不保证送达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to keep history linear and catch it up to main before a PR. Merge when others already have the branch or you want the record of when work joined.
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change is written down before it's applied to the actual data, so that if the system crashes midway, it can replay the log and recover without losing committed work. It trades a small write cost for durability and fast crash recovery.
```

  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="ss">每天早上更新</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<script>
var LIMIT = 600;
function fmt(n){ return '¥' + n; }
function render(d){
  var trains = (d && d.trains) || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  var under = trains.filter(function(t){ return t.price < LIMIT; });
  trains.forEach(function(t){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = t.no;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = t.seats != null ? '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '';
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = fmt(t.price) + (t.price < LIMIT ? '' : '（超 600）');
    val.style.color = t.price < LIMIT ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)';
    row.appendChild(main); row.appendChild(sub); row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if (!trains.length) {
    list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没有班次，明早会更新。</div>';
  }
  var sv = document.getElementById('sv');
  var ss = document.getElementById('ss');
  if (under.length) {
    var low = under.reduce(function(a, b){ return a.price <= b.price ? a : b; });
    sv.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    var tight = under.filter(function(t){ return t.seats != null && t.seats <= 5; });
    ss.textContent = '最低 ' + fmt(low.price) + ' · ' + low.no +
      (tight.length ? ' 只剩 ' + tight[0].seats + ' 张' : '');
  } else if (trains.length) {
    sv.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">暂无</span>';
    ss.textContent = '今天没有 600 元以下的班次';
  }
}
var got = ducter.get('trains');
if (got && got.then) { got.then(render); } else { render(got); }
ducter.onChange(render);
</script>
</main>
; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}

(function () {
  function render() {
    var nights = ducter.get('nights') || [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!nights.length) {
      empty.style.display = '';
      return;
    }
    empty.style.display = 'none';
    var latest = nights[nights.length - 1];
    document.getElementById('sum-writes').textContent = latest.writes + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('sum-sub').textContent =
      latest.night + ' · p99 ' + latest.p99 + ' · ' + latest.stalls + ' compaction stalls';
    nights.slice().reverse().forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML =
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + n.night + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · ' + n.stalls + ' compaction stalls</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + n.writes + '/s</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();

  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2254 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
let cat="交通";
const esc=s=>String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,c=>({"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;"}[c]));
const money=n=>"¥"+Number(n||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const sum=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.amt||0),0);
  $("total").innerHTML=xs.length?money(sum):'<span class="k-none">还没记</span>';
  $("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记一笔就会算合计";
  $("list").innerHTML=xs.length?xs.map(x=>'<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+(x.from?' <span class="k-from">'+esc(x.from)+'</span>':'')+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day||"")+' · '+esc(x.cat||"")+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+money(x.amt)+'</div></div>').join(""):'<p class="k-empty">还没有花销，记一笔就会出现在这里</p>';
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+money(sum):"还没记");
}
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{
  cat=b.dataset.c;
  document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(c=>c.setAttribute("aria-pressed",c===b?"true":"false"));
});
$("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=$("amt").value.trim();
  if(!$("trip").value.trim()||!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim(),day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:Number(amt)});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("trip").value="";$("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"from":"你的预订"},{"trip":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"from":"你的预订"}]}
